public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsToeplitzMatrix {
    public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        if (mat.length == 0) return true;
        int n = mat.length, m = mat[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            for (int j = 1; j < m; j++) {
                if (mat[i][j] != mat[i - 1][j - 1]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsToeplitzMatrix {
    //@ requires mat != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == (\forall int i, j; 1 <= i < mat.length && 1 <= j < mat[0].length; mat[i][j] == mat[i - 1][j - 1]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires mat != null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ pure
    public static boolean isToeplitzMatrix(int[][] mat) {
        if (mat.length == 0) return true;
        int n = mat.length, m = mat[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= n;
        //@ loop_invariant \forall int k, j; 1 <= k < i && 1 <= j < mat[0].length; mat[k][j] == mat[k - 1][j - 1];
        //@ loop_writes \nothing;
        //@ decreases n - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 1 <= j <= m;
            //@ loop_invariant \forall int k; 1 <= k < i && 1 <= j < m; mat[k][j] == mat[k - 1][j - 1];
            //@ loop_writes \nothing;
            //@ decreases m - j;
            for (int j = 1; j < m; j++) {
                if (mat[i][j] != mat[i - 1][j - 1]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }
}

    //@ requires mat != null;
    //@ requires mat.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ pure

        if (mat.length == 0) return true;
